    Legislation
                                         of the



        Virgin Islands
                                          as at


                             1st January 2026




                   Compiled and published under the
                Law Revision/Accessibility of Laws Project
      Attorney General’s Chambers, Government of the Virgin Islands

 © 2026 Attorney General’s Chambers, Government of the Virgin Islands. All rights reserved.
                                                  User Guide

      The legislation appearing in the Consolidated Index represents legislation enacted in, or
      extended to, the Virgin Islands as at 1stJanuary, 2026. The Consolidated Index includes
      legislation found in the 1961, 1991, 2013, 2020 and 2023 Revised Editions of the Laws of the
      Virgin Islands, together with Acts and Subsidiary Legislation enacted between 1990 to 1st
      January, 2026, which have not been revised; together with U.K. Imperial Legislation that
      has been extended to the Territory.

      Here are a few examples of what to look for:
                                                                                  This column shows the location of the law

                                                       -ii-
Acts:
Acts will appear indicating the title and the enactment number, together with the date the
Act came into force. If the Act provided for special commencement procedures e.g.
specified in a section of the Act, or by a Notice or Proclamation, the section or citation for
the Instrument will appear after the with effect date (w.e.f.); as follows:

 BENEFICIAL OWNERSHIP SECURE SEARCH SYSTEM ACT                             2020 Rev. Ed.
 Act 15/2017 (w.e.f. 30.06.17 by sec. 1(2)) Commencement
                                                    date & section

 ANTI-DISCRIMINATION ACT, 2001                                               Act 2/2001
 (w.e.f. 15.11.2006 by S.I 68/2006)                 Commencement
                                                    date &
                                                    Commencement
                                                    Instrument No.


Revised Acts:
Where an Act has been revised, it will appear indicating the title and most recent revised
date. Immediately below the title, will appear the original enactment number “Act
3/1881”, together with the date the Act came into force “(w.e.f.)”; as follows:
                                                                                     Revised Edition date

 ABOLITION OF MINIMUM PUNISHMENTS ACT                                      2023 Rev. Ed.
 Act 3/1881 (w.e.f. 01.03.1882)


Amended Acts:
Where an Act has been amended, the amendment(s) will be listed chronologically after the
title, citing the instrument (whether Act or S.I.) together with the w.e.f. date which is the
source of the amendment. This will appear in red; as follows:

 ADMINISTRATION OF SMALL ESTATES ACT (CAP. 4)                              1991 Rev. Ed.        Amending Act No.,
                                                                                                Commencement
 Act 13/1944 (w.e.f. 29.07.1944)                                                                date &
 Amended by: Act 10/2021 (w.e.f. 09.07.2021 by S.I. 86/2021)                                    Commencement
                                                                                                Instrument


Where an Act has been amended, by an Act that is not an amending Act of that Act, the
name of the amending Act will be included for ease of reference; as follows:

 ADOPTION OF CHILDREN ACT (CAP. 269)                                       1991 Rev. Ed.
 Act 18/1944 (w.e.f. 13.12.1944)                                                              Amending Act name,
                                                                                              Commencement date
 Amended by: Act 21/1994 - The Age of Majority Act, 1994 (w.e.f.                              & Commencement
                                                                                              Instrument
               03.01.1995 by S.I. 42/1994)




                                            -iii-
Acts with Subsidiary legislation:
Where Subsidiary Legislation has been made pursuant to an Act, the Subsidiary
Legislation will be listed citing the section under which it has been made in blue; as follows:

 ANTIBIOTICS AND THERAPEUTIC SUBSTANCES ACT                                 1991 Rev. Ed.
 (CAP. 176)                                                                                  Subsidiary
                                                                                             Legislation
 Act 15/1951 (w.e.f. 01.06.1951)
 Sec. 15:     Antibiotics and Therapeutic Substances Regulations            1991 Rev. Ed.


U.K. Legislation:
Applicable U.K. Imperial Legislation will appear with the letters “(U.K.)” after the title.
The information with respect to the extension of the legislation to the Virgin Islands
together with the U.K. citation will follow immediately below. Where the legislation can
be found in a local document, the reference will be included in the reference column.

 ADMINISTRATION OF JUSTICE ACT 1920 (U.K.) (PT. II)                         1991 Rev. Ed.
 UK 1920 c. 81
 Sec. 14:     Part II Extended to the Virgin Islands by:                                       Extended
                                                                                               Legislation
              Reciprocal Enforcement of Judgments (Administration of
              Justice Act 1920, Part II) (Consolidation) Order UKSI 1984
              No. 129




                                             -iv-
                              KEY TO COLOURED TEXT

RED          Amendments to an Act
BLUE         Subsidiary legislation and amendments to subsidiary legislation
GREY         Information in respect of name changes, revocations, repeals, not in force
             etc.


                                   ABBREVIATIONS

Ann. Vol.    Annual Volumes
CAP.         Chapter reference in respect of the 1961 and 1991 Revised Editions
Rev. Ed.     Revised Edition
S.I.         Statutory Instrument
UKSI         UK Statutory Instrument
w.e.f.       With effect from


A few other things to note:

   •   Dates appear as day.month.year
   •   If a commencement date is not cited, the law came into force as per the operation
       of section 16 of the Interpretation Act, CAP. 136
